I found this vintage bowling shirt once that had the name \"Earl\" embroidered on the pocket. I wore it to a Halloween party last year and told everyone I was a professional bowler. I even learned how to keep score on paper, which is surprisingly complicated with all the strikes and spares and frames...\n\nBut um... yeah, no, I haven't ever thought about doing anything to hurt myself on purpose. Just... just the usual clumsiness, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble concentrating on things?\n\nPatient: Um... concentrating? I mean, maybe a little? It's hard to say. I guess I do zone out sometimes when I'm working. \n\nLike yesterday, I was supposed to be running this data analysis for my manager. We use this software that takes forever to load, so you just sit there watching this little loading bar creep across the screen. It reminds me of back when we had dial-up internet at my mom's house. You'd hear that awful screeching sound, and then it would take ten minutes just to load a single picture. My brother and I used to fight over the phone line all the time because he always wanted to call his girlfriend right when I was trying to play Runescape. He actually just married that girl last month. They had this huge outdoor wedding, but it rained the entire time. The caterers had to move all the food into this tiny tent, and everyone was bumping into each other trying to get to the buffet. They served these little crab cakes that were actually pretty good, though. I'm usually not a big seafood guy. I got food poisoning from some bad shrimp at a buffet once in college, and it completely ruined it for me. I couldn't even look at the ocean for like a year without feeling a little sick to my stomach...\n\nBut um... yeah, I guess I do get distracted sometimes. Um... before? I don't really know. I mean, maybe? I guess I've just felt kind of... like this for a long time, so it's hard to say if there were specific periods or if it's just always been there. It just feels like a blur.

Speaking of blurs, I forgot to wear my glasses today. I really need to go to the optometrist and get my prescription checked. The last time I went, the doctor had this machine that blew a puff of air right into my eyeball. I hate that thing. One of them was a tiny avocado with a smiley face on it. It reminded me of this avocado toast I had at a brunch place downtown a few weeks ago. It was ridiculous—they charged like eighteen dollars for one slice of bread and half an avocado, and they didn't even toast the bread all the way through. It was just soggy in the middle. I even started keeping a spreadsheet of my attempts, because, you know, I'm an analyst, I can't help it. And I found this forum post from 2018 where this guy was convinced the drop rate was tied to the in-game clock, so I tried syncing my attempts to midnight server time, which meant I was staying up till like 3 AM on a Tuesday. My roommate at the time got so annoyed because my mechanical keyboard is really loud. He actually threatened to pour coffee on it once. But yeah... I don't know, I guess I just don't play as much anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you get as much pleasure from playing video games as you used to?\n\nPatient: Um... I'm not really sure, honestly. Like I said, I haven't really thought about if it's less fun or whatever. I just... I haven't been playing much. Actually, my PlayStation controller broke a couple of weeks ago. The right bumper just completely snapped off. I was playing this racing game and I guess I hit it too hard, but honestly, the build quality on those controllers has gone way downhill since the PS3 era. I tried looking up how to fix it on YouTube, and this guy had a whole 40-minute tutorial, but he was using all these specialized tiny screwdrivers that I don't have. I went to Home Depot to see if I could buy a kit, but the guy working in the hardware section was completely useless. He tried to sell me a standard Phillips head set, and I was like, dude, do you even know what a Torx screw is? It was so frustrating. I ended up just ordering a new controller on Amazon, but it got delayed in shipping because of some weather thing in the Midwest. So yeah... I don't know if I enjoy games less. I just haven't been playing.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest in things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really think so. I was making these pancakes from scratch—not from a mix, because I watched this cooking show where they explained how the chemical leaveners in the boxed mixes can leave a weird metallic aftertaste. So I bought buttermilk and everything. But she called right as I was pouring the batter into the pan, and my phone was on the counter, so I had to answer it on speaker. And she started going on and on about how my cousin just bought a boat, and how he's always out on the lake having fun, and she was like, \"Why don't you do fun things anymore?\" And I was trying to explain to her that I just didn't want a boat, but then I got distracted and the first batch of pancakes completely burned. Like, black on the bottom. It set off the smoke detector, which is super sensitive because it's right near the kitchen door, and my cat freaked out and hid under the sofa for like three hours. I tried playing it once but I just kept dying in this virtual cave because I couldn't figure out how to light a lantern. \n\nBut um... yeah, what was I saying? Oh, right. With work. It just feels like every single time I miss a follow-up or send an email a little late, it's just undeniable proof that I'm not actually as capable as they all think I am.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought about death or wanted to die?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I mean, I don't sit around wishing I was dead or anything like that. I just... I don't know, sometimes I just feel really tired, you know? Like that kind of tired where sleep doesn't actually fix it. \n\nI was actually trying to fix my sleep schedule last week because I read this whole thread online about circadian rhythms and how blue light from screens messes with your melatonin production. So I bought these blue-light-blocking glasses off Amazon. They have these really thick, orange lenses, and I look absolutely ridiculous wearing them. My roommate walked in while I was watching TV with them on and he just started laughing so hard he spilled his water. Which reminds me, we still haven't gotten our water filter fixed. The landlord said he was going to send a guy out on Tuesday, but nobody ever showed up. It's so frustrating because we have to keep buying those big plastic jugs from the grocery store, and they're so heavy to carry up three flights of stairs. I usually try to carry two at a time to save trips, but the plastic handles dig into my hands so badly. I should probably get some of those grip pads they use for weightlifting. My buddy from high school got really into powerlifting recently, and his entire Instagram feed is just him deadlifting in his garage... \n\nBut um... yeah, no, I haven't really had any thoughts about dying or anything like that. Just... just tired mostly.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty or worthless about things you've done or haven't done?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess I do feel guilty sometimes. Like I'm just not doing enough, you know? Especially with my friends. I keep blowing off plans because I just don't have the energy to go out. \n\nLike last weekend, my buddy Dave invited me to this big barbecue he was hosting. He just bought a new house out in the suburbs, one of those places with a huge backyard and a built-in fire pit. He's been talking about this fire pit for months. He even bought this special kind of wood that's supposed to smell like cedar when it burns. I don't know much about wood, honestly. My grandpa used to whittle these little wooden birds out of pine when I was a kid. He had this whole collection of them lined up on his mantelpiece, right next to this old grandfather clock that chimed every hour. The chimes used to scare me when I stayed over, especially at night. It's funny how things that seem so loud when you're little don't really bother you when you get older. Kind of like how I used to hate the taste of black coffee, but now I drink it every morning. I started going to this new coffee shop near my apartment, actually. The barista there has this crazy blue hair, like neon blue... \n\nBut um... what was the question? Oh, right, feeling guilty. Yeah, I just... I feel bad that I keep canceling on people, but I just can't seem to make myself go.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: Um... no. No, I haven't wanted to hurt myself or anything like that. I don't really get thoughts like that. \n\nI did actually cut my hand pretty bad the other day, though, but it was a total accident. I was trying to open one of those hard plastic blister packs that literally everything seems to come in nowadays. It was for a new computer mouse, actually. My old one started doing this weird double-clicking thing every time I pressed the left button, which was driving me absolutely insane when I was trying to highlight cells in Excel. Do you know how hard it is to build a pivot table when your mouse keeps unselecting everything? Anyway, I was using these kitchen shears to open the packaging, and they slipped. They're these really heavy-duty shears I got from a thrift store a few years ago. I love thrift stores, you can find the most random stuff there. I found this vintage bowling shirt once that had the name \"Earl\" embroidered on the pocket. I wore it to a Halloween party last year and told everyone I was a professional bowler. I even learned how to keep score on paper, which is surprisingly complicated with all the strikes and spares and frames...\n\nBut um... yeah, no, I haven't ever thought about doing anything to hurt myself on purpose. Just... just the usual clumsiness, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble concentrating on things?\n\nPatient: Um... concentrating? I mean, maybe a little? It's hard to say. I guess I do zone out sometimes when I'm working. \n\nLike yesterday, I was supposed to be running this data analysis for my manager. We use this software that takes forever to load, so you just sit there watching this little loading bar creep across the screen. It reminds me of back when we had dial-up internet at my mom's house. You'd hear that awful screeching sound, and then it would take ten minutes just to load a single picture. My brother and I used to fight over the phone line all the time because he always wanted to call his girlfriend right when I was trying to play Runescape. He actually just married that girl last month. They had this huge outdoor wedding, but it rained the entire time. The caterers had to move all the food into this tiny tent, and everyone was bumping into each other trying to get to the buffet. They served these little crab cakes that were actually pretty good, though. I'm usually not a big seafood guy. I got food poisoning from some bad shrimp at a buffet once in college, and it completely ruined it for me. I couldn't even look at the ocean for like a year without feeling a little sick to my stomach...\n\nBut um... yeah, I guess I do get distracted sometimes. I don't know if it's worse than normal, though. Just... my mind wanders, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself moving or speaking more slowly than you used to, or do you feel restless and unable to sit still?\n\nPatient: Um... moving slowly? My old roommate had one of those exercise balls instead of a chair. He was super into fitness, always talking about core strength and macros and stuff. He used to make these protein shakes that smelled like wet dog and chalk. He tried to get me to drink one once, and I swear I couldn't taste anything else for the rest of the day. We used to live in this tiny apartment above a dry cleaner. You could always smell the chemicals coming up through the floorboards, especially in the summer when it got really hot. We didn't have AC, just this one box fan that we'd stick in the window. It made this loud rattling noise, but it was better than nothing. I actually saw him the other day, my old roommate I mean. He was at the grocery store, buying like, five cartons of eggs. I don't know who needs that many eggs at once...\n\nBut um... what were we talking about? Oh, right. No, I don't think I'm moving slower or feeling restless or anything. Just... just normal, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're able to move around and do physical things at the same pace as you used to?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I think so? I mean, my pace feels about the same as it always has. I haven't really noticed feeling slower or anything when I'm doing physical stuff. \n\nI actually tried to start running a couple weeks ago. I went to the mall and bought these really expensive running shoes. They're bright neon green. The guy at the shoe store said they reflect light for night running, which I guess is safe, but they're so bright they kind of hurt your eyes in the daylight. He was this really intense guy, too, kept talking about his marathon times and his resting heart rate. He kind of reminded me of my high school gym teacher who used to make us run bleachers in the rain. I absolutely hated the bleachers. Once I slipped and scraped my shin so bad on the metal edge that I still have a scar. It actually looks a little bit like the state of Florida if you squint at it right. I've never even been to Florida, though. My aunt lives down in Tampa and she keeps inviting me to visit, but she has like six cats and I'm super allergic. The last time I was around a cat, my eyes swelled up so bad I looked like I had gone ten rounds in a boxing ring. I had to take Benadryl and just sleep for like fourteen hours straight... \n\nBut um... wait, what was the question? Oh, doing physical things. Yeah, no, I think I move at a pretty normal pace. Nothing's really changed there.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel worthless?\n\nPatient: Um... worthless? I don't know if I'd use that exact word, but I definitely feel like a fraud a lot of the time. Um... no, no I haven't ever thought about hurting myself or... wishing I was dead. I mean, things feel kind of heavy and dull a lot, but I've never actually wanted to... you know, end it or anything. 

It's kind of like... I don't know, like when my car wouldn't start last winter. I have this old Honda Civic, and the battery just completely died on the coldest day of the year. I was stuck in the parking lot at work for like three hours waiting for a tow truck. The tow truck driver was this huge guy with a giant beard, and he was listening to the most intense heavy metal music I've ever heard. He told me he plays bass in a band on the weekends and they were going on tour in Europe. I always wonder how people afford to do stuff like that. Like, touring Europe has to be so expensive, right? I've only been out of the country once, to Mexico for a friend's wedding. It was supposed to be this beautiful beach ceremony, but a hurricane rolled in the day before and we had to do the whole thing in the hotel conference room. It smelled like stale coffee and chlorine from the indoor pool next door. They tried to make it nice with all these fake palm trees, but it just looked like a high school prom. My suit got completely ruined on the walk from my room to the conference center because the wind was blowing the rain sideways. I tried to get it dry cleaned when I got back, but the dry cleaner near my apartment closed down. They turned it into one of those fancy dog bakeries where they sell cupcakes made out of liver and sweet potatoes...

Wait, sorry. I'm doing it again, aren't I? What was the question? Oh, right, thoughts of hurting myself. Yeah, no. I don't have thoughts like that.

I'm only 22, I haven't even finished my college degree yet, so having all these senior guys staring at me and expecting me to speak was just... a lot. My manager meant well, but she kept scheduling me for all these cross-departmental presentations after that. I even had to buy a new suit for one of them, and the tailor totally messed up the hem on the pants, so I had to go back to the mall twice just to get it fixed before the presentation. It was a whole annoying ordeal. But yeah... ever since that whole dashboard thing blew up, I've just had this constant heavy feeling.\n\nDoctor: Do you still enjoy activities or hobbies that you used to find pleasurable before this flat, heavy feeling started?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know if I enjoy them less. I guess I haven't really thought about it. I used to play a lot of video games, mostly these really long RPGs where you have to grind for hours just to get a specific sword or whatever. I spent like three weeks trying to get this one rare drop in Final Fantasy, and the drop rate was supposed to be 5%, but I swear the RNG was broken because I killed the same boss probably two hundred times. And I found this forum post from 2018 where this guy was convinced the drop rate was tied to the in-game clock, so I tried syncing my attempts to midnight server time, which meant I was staying up till like 3 AM on a Tuesday. My roommate at the time got so annoyed because my mechanical keyboard is really loud. He actually threatened to pour coffee on it once. But yeah... The right bumper just completely snapped off. I was playing this racing game and I guess I hit it too hard, but honestly, the build quality on those controllers has gone way downhill since the PS3 era. I tried looking up how to fix it on YouTube, and this guy had a whole 40-minute tutorial, but he was using all these specialized tiny screwdrivers that I don't have. I went to Home Depot to see if I could buy a kit, but the guy working in the hardware section was completely useless. He tried to sell me a standard Phillips head set, and I was like, dude, do you even know what a Torx screw is? It was so frustrating. I ended up just ordering a new controller on Amazon, but it got delayed in shipping because of some weather thing in the Midwest. So yeah... I don't know if I enjoy games less. I just haven't been playing.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest in things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really think so. But yeah, it's not that I lost interest in trivia or anything, it's just... circumstances, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like things that used to give you pleasure no longer do?\n\nPatient: Um... no, I don't think so. It's funny you ask that though, because I was actually just talking to my mom about pleasure and stuff the other day. She called me on Sunday morning, right when I was trying to make breakfast. I was making these pancakes from scratch—not from a mix, because I watched this cooking show where they explained how the chemical leaveners in the boxed mixes can leave a weird metallic aftertaste. So I bought buttermilk and everything. But she called right as I was pouring the batter into the pan, and my phone was on the counter, so I had to answer it on speaker. And she started going on and on about how my cousin just bought a boat, and how he's always out on the lake having fun, and she was like, \"Why don't you do fun things anymore?\" And I was trying to explain to her that I just didn't want a boat, but then I got distracted and the first batch of pancakes completely burned. Like, black on the bottom. It set off the smoke detector, which is super sensitive because it's right near the kitchen door, and my cat freaked out and hid under the sofa for like three hours. My buddy Steve had that happen with his fridge, and he ended up having to live out of a cooler for a week while he waited for a new one to get delivered. But yeah... The other night I started watching this video about how they restore old cast iron skillets. It's actually crazy how rusty they can get, and these guys use electrolysis tanks to strip all the gunk off. They moved the export button from the top right to the bottom left, which makes zero sense from a design perspective.
